- **bwa-mem**: Map Illumina paired-end reads (70-100bp) to reference genomes using BWA-MEM alignment ([DNALinux/tonina](https://github.com/DNALinux/tonina/tree/HEAD/skills/hermes/bwamem) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **bwa-mem2**: Architecture-aware faster BWA-MEM for Illumina reads to contigs/genomes. Use when a BWA-MEM workflow is required but throughput must improve, or when migrating from classic bwa mem. Index format is NOT compatible with classic bwa index. ([PKU-EMBL/Metagenomics-Skills](https://github.com/PKU-EMBL/Metagenomics-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bwa-mem2) · ★7)
- **bwa-mem2-alignment**: Use BWA-MEM2 — the SIMD-accelerated BWA-MEM replacement — for 2-3x speedup on human genome alignment with identical results to BWA-MEM. ([pradyumnasagar/open-research-skills](https://github.com/pradyumnasagar/open-research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bioinformatics-sequence/bwa-mem2-alignment) · ★8)
- **bwa-mem2-dna-aligner**: Fast short-read DNA aligner for WGS/WES/ChIP-seq. 2× faster BWA-MEM successor; outputs SAM/BAM with read group headers for GATK. Primary plus supplementary records for chimeric reads. Use STAR for RNA-seq splice-aware alignment; Bowtie2 is a comparable alternative. ([jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills](https://github.com/jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genomics-bioinformatics/alignment/bwa-mem2-dna-aligner) · ★394 · 5 copies)
- **bwa-mem2-dna-aligner**: 当需要把 Illumina 短读 DNA（50–250 bp，WGS/WES/ChIP-seq/ATAC-seq）比对到参考基因组、生成带 @RG 读组头的排序 BAM 供 GATK/Picard 变异检测或 peak calling 时使用；用 bwa-mem2 index 建索引、bwa-mem2 mem 比对并管道接 samtools sort/index、再 markdup 去重，产出 sorted/markdup BAM 与 flagstat 质控；不适用于跨剪接位点的 RNA-seq（用 star-rnaseq-aligner）、&lt;50bp 极短读（用 BWA-backtrack）、长读（用 minimap2）。触发词：bwa-mem2、DNA 比对、短读、WGS、WES、ChIP-seq、ATAC-seq、@RG 读组、BAM、samtools sort、markdup、参考基因组比对 ([findscripter/everything-skills](https://github.com/findscripter/everything-skills/tree/HEAD/09-verticals/bwa-mem2-dna-aligner) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **bwa-mem2-dna-aligner**: Fast short-read DNA aligner for whole-genome, whole-exome, and ChIP-seq alignment to a reference genome. BWA-MEM2 is the 2× faster successor to BWA-MEM; outputs SAM/BAM with read group headers required by GATK. Produces primary alignments with supplementary records for chimeric reads. Use STAR instead for RNA-seq splice-aware alignment; use Bowtie2 as an alternative with comparable accuracy. ([sulfierry/mcp](https://github.com/sulfierry/mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/bwa-mem2-dna-aligner) · ★1)
- **bwa-mismatch-penalty-tuning**: Use when reads must be recruited to a target virus via a distant reference genome and you need to sweep BWA mismatch penalties (e.g., -B 1/2/4/8) to build seed-read sets for overlap-based recruitment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/bwa-mismatch-penalty-tuning))
- **bwa-monatsabschluss-routine**: Wenn es um Monatsabschluss-Routine für den Steuerberater in Steuerrecht – Steuerberater und Anwälte geht: ordnet Akteninhalt, Belege, Lücken und Nachforderungen; liefert eine Schnittstellenkarte mit Kollisions-, Zuständigkeits- und Nachweisfragen. - **bwa-quartal**: Wenn es um Quartalsgespraech — BWA-Übergabe mit dem Mandanten in Steuerrecht – Steuerberater und Anwälte geht: prüft Frist, Form, Zuständigkeit, Rechtsweg und Sofortmaßnahmen; liefert eine Fristen- und Risikoampel mit Sofortschritten. - **BYL-cptsd-toolkit**: 当用户带着 CPTSD / 童年创伤相关的困扰来求助时使用——一个 skill 覆盖全书十个核心方法，用户无需在多个 skill 间切换。先按主诉导航定位模块，再执行对应步骤。覆盖：情绪闪回急救、4F 反应类型识别、内在批判者攻击与纠正、思维阻断、哀悼四方式、遗弃抑郁、自我再抚育、足够好原则、界限与权利、从自己开始的原谅。若诉求超出本书自助范畴（确诊抑郁需用药、现实家暴需报警、纯科普），明确边界并给外部方向。关键 trigger 词：我又闪回了 / 不知道该用哪个方法 / 脑子里有声音骂我 / 想哭不敢哭 / 不敢拒绝 / 被逼原谅 / 觉得自己永远不够好 / 童年创伤该从哪入手。 ([magiclee-syxk/BYL-cptsd-skills](https://github.com/magiclee-syxk/BYL-cptsd-skills/tree/HEAD/BYL-cptsd-toolkit) · ★1)
